CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION IAS_Get_Prompt_By_Fld (P_Lng_no In number ,
P_CLM_nm In char ,
P_TBL_nm In char DEFAULT '0') RETURN CHAR Is
Cmnt varchar2(100);
BEGIN
Select Ias_Gen_Pkg.Get_Prompt(P_Lng_no,TO_NUMBER(SUBSTR(Comments,1,4)))
into Cmnt
From User_Col_Comments
Where Comments In (Select To_Char(Label_No) From Ias_Labels)
AND DECODE(P_TBL_nm,'0','0',Table_Name )=NVL(P_TBL_nm,'0')
AND COLUMN_NAME=P_clm_nm
And RowNum<=1;
--IF Cmnt LIKE 'Cash/Bank' OR Cmnt LIKE '
' THEN
IF Cmnt LIKE IAS_GEN_PKG.GET_PROMPT(P_Lng_no,1220) THEN
CMNT:=IAS_GEN_PKG.GET_PROMPT(P_Lng_no,3183);
END IF;
Return(Cmnt);
Exception
When Others Then
Return(Null);
END IAS_Get_Prompt_By_Fld;
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION Get_Ac_Bal_Fnc( P_a_code In ACCOUNT.A_CODE%TYPE ,
P_post In Number Default 1) RETURN NUMBER IS
v_bl_amt NUMBER:=0;
If P_a_code Is Not Null Then --(1)
Select Round(nvl(sum(nvl(amt,0)),0) ,2)
Into v_bl_amt
From ias_post_dtl d
where nvl(Doc_Post,0) = Decode (P_post ,2,nvl(Doc_Post,0),1)
and (d.a_code In (Select a_code from ias_Acc_tree where AC_TREE = P_a_code ) Or d.a_code = p_a_code)
and nvl(doc_post,0) = Decode(P_post,2,nvl(doc_post,0),P_post);
End If; --(1)
Return(nvl(v_bl_amt,0));
When others then
Return(0);
End Get_Ac_Bal_Fnc;
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION GET_AC_TYPE(P_a_code In ACCOUNT.A_CODE%TYPE) RETURN NUMBER IS
r_bs NUMBER;
Begin
Select nvl(report_bs,0)
Into r_bs
From account_report_type,account
where account_report_type.report_type=account.a_report
and account.a_code=P_a_code;
Return(r_bs);
when others then
End GET_AC_TYPE;
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION Doc_Type_Name( P_lang In NUMBER,
P_doctype In NUMBER,
P_jvtype In NUMBER Default Null) RETURN CHAR Is
v_jvname VARCHAR2(100);
v_dvname VARCHAR2(50);
If P_doctype = 0 Then
Return (Ias_Gen_Pkg.Get_Prompt (P_lang,1647));
ElsIf P_jvtype Is Null Then
Select DOC_TYPE_NAME
Into v_jvname
From Ias_Sys.IAS_DOCJV_TYPE_SYSTEMS_MST
Where Lang_no = P_lang
and DOC_TYPE = P_Doctype;
Return(v_jvname);
Return(P_doctype);
End;
Elsif P_Doctype = 1 Then
Select Decode(P_Lang,1,jv_name,jv_e_name)
From jv_types
where jv_type=P_jvtype;
Elsif P_Doctype = 8 then
Select Decode(P_Lang,1,incom_name,incom_e_name)
From Incom_Types
where Incom_Type=P_jvtype;
return(P_doctype);
Elsif P_Doctype=9 then
Select Decode(P_Lang,1,out_name,out_e_name)
From out_Types
where out_Type=P_jvtype;
Elsif P_Doctype = 11 then
Select Decode(P_Lang,1,Tr_name,Tr_e_name)
From transfer_Types
where Tr_Type=P_jvtype;
Return(P_Doctype);
ElsIf P_jvtype Is Not Null Then
Select Flg_Desc
Into v_dvname
From S_Flags
and Flg_Code ='POST_DOC_TYPE'
and Flg_Value= P_doctype;
v_dvname:=Null;
If P_Doctype In (2,3) Then
and Flg_Code ='CASH_CHQ'
and Flg_Value= P_jvtype;
v_jvname:=Null;
ElsIf P_Doctype In (4,5,6,7) Then
and Flg_Code ='TYPE_NAME_SI'
ElsIf P_Doctype = 10 Then
and Flg_Code ='STK_ADJ_TYPE'
ElsIf P_Doctype >=60 Then
Select Jv_NAME
From Ias_Sys.IAS_DOCJV_TYPE_SYSTEMS
and DOC_TYPE = P_Doctype
and JV_TYPE = P_jvtype;
Select Doc_Type_Name
End If;
Return(v_dvname||' '||v_jvname);
Else
END Doc_Type_Name;
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION GET_VER_CODE_NAME( P_Lng_no In Number DEFAULT 1,
P_CLM_NAM IN VARCHAR2,
P_CLM_VAL IN VARCHAR2,
P_Ac_Dtl_Typ IN NUMBER DEFAULT 0
)RETURN VARCHAR2 IS
V_Name VARCHAR2(250);
P_CLM VARCHAR2(60);
P_CLM :=''||P_CLM_NAM||'';
IF UPPER(P_CLM)='A_CODE' THEN
V_Name:=IAS_Acode_Pkg.Get_A_Name(P_CLM_VAL,P_Lng_no);
Elsif UPPER(P_CLM)='C_CODE' THEN
V_Name:=IAS_Cst_Pkg.Get_C_Name(P_CLM_VAL,P_Lng_no);
Elsif UPPER(P_CLM)='V_CODE' THEN
V_Name:=IAS_Vndr_Pkg.Get_v_Name(P_CLM_VAL,P_Lng_no);
Elsif UPPER(P_CLM)='CASH_NO' THEN
V_Name:=IAS_CshBnk_Pkg.Get_CB_Name (1,P_CLM_VAL,P_Lng_no);
Elsif UPPER(P_CLM)='BANK_NO' THEN
V_Name:=IAS_CshBnk_Pkg.Get_CB_Name (2,P_CLM_VAL,P_Lng_no);
Elsif UPPER(P_CLM)='EMP_NO' THEN
V_Name:=YS_EMP_PKG.Get_EMP_NM (P_CLM_VAL,P_Lng_no);
Elsif UPPER(P_CLM)='CC_CODE' THEN
V_Name:=IAS_Cc_Code_Pkg.Get_Cc_Name(P_CLM_VAL,P_Lng_no);
Elsif UPPER(P_CLM)='PJ_NO' THEN
V_Name:=IAS_PJ_Pkg.Get_PJ_Name(P_CLM_VAL,P_Lng_no);
Elsif UPPER(P_CLM)='ACTV_NO' THEN
V_Name:=IAS_Actv_Pkg.Get_Actv_Name(P_CLM_VAL,P_Lng_no);
Elsif UPPER(P_CLM)='BRN_NO' THEN
V_Name:=IAS_BRN_PKG.Get_Br_Nm(P_CLM_VAL,P_Lng_no);
Elsif UPPER(P_CLM)='REP_CODE' THEN
Select Decode(P_Lng_no,1,Nvl(REPRS_A_NAME,REPRS_E_NAME) ,Nvl(REPRS_E_NAME,REPRS_A_NAME) )
InTo V_Name
From Sales_man
Where REPRS_CODE=P_CLM_VAL And RowNum<=1;
Elsif UPPER(P_CLM)='COL_NO' THEN
Select Decode(P_Lng_no,1,Nvl(COL_NAME,COL_E_NAME) ,Nvl(COL_E_NAME,COL_NAME) )
From COLLERCTOR
Where COL_NO=P_CLM_VAL And RowNum<=1;
Elsif UPPER(P_CLM)='R_CODE' THEN
Select Decode(P_Lng_no,1,Nvl(R_A_NAME,R_E_NAME), Nvl(R_E_NAME,R_A_NAME) )
From REGIONS
Where R_CODE=P_CLM_VAL And RowNum<=1;
Elsif UPPER(P_CLM)='CNTRY_NO' THEN
Select Decode(P_lng_no,1,Nvl(CNTRY_A_NAME,CNTRY_E_NAME),Nvl(CNTRY_E_NAME,CNTRY_A_NAME))
From CNTRY
Where CNTRY_NO=P_CLM_VAL And RowNum<=1;
Elsif UPPER(P_CLM)='PROV_NO' THEN
Select Decode(P_lng_no,1,Nvl(PROV_A_NAME,PROV_E_NAME),Nvl(PROV_E_NAME,PROV_A_NAME))
From IAS_PROVINCES
Where PROV_NO=P_CLM_VAL And RowNum<=1;
Elsif UPPER(P_CLM)='CITY_NO' THEN
Select Decode(P_lng_no,1,Nvl(CITY_A_NAME,CITY_E_NAME),Nvl(CITY_E_NAME,CITY_A_NAME))
From CITIES
Where CITY_NO=P_CLM_VAL And RowNum<=1;
Elsif UPPER(P_CLM)='CMP_NO' THEN
Select Decode(P_lng_no,1,Nvl(CMP_LNAME,CMP_FNAME),Nvl(CMP_FNAME,CMP_LNAME))
From S_CMPNY
Where CMP_NO=P_CLM_VAL And RowNum<=1;
Elsif UPPER(P_CLM)='LC_NO' THEN
Select LC_DESC
From LC_MASTER
Where LC_NO=P_CLM_VAL And RowNum<=1;
Elsif UPPER(P_CLM)='ZONE_NO' THEN
Select Decode(P_Lng_no,1,Nvl(ZONE_L_NM,ZONE_F_NM), Nvl(ZONE_F_NM,ZONE_L_NM) )
From S_ZONE
Where ZONE_NO=P_CLM_VAL And RowNum<=1;
Elsif UPPER(P_CLM)='AC_CODE_DTL' THEN
--P_Cb_Type
If P_Ac_Dtl_Typ in(1,2) then
V_Name:=IAS_CshBnk_Pkg.Get_CB_Name (P_Ac_Dtl_Typ,P_CLM_VAL,P_Lng_no);
ElsIf P_Ac_Dtl_Typ=3 Then
ElsIf P_Ac_Dtl_Typ=4 Then
ElsIf P_Ac_Dtl_Typ =7 Then
V_Name:=YS_EMP_Pkg.Get_EMP_NM(P_CLM_VAL,P_Lng_no);
-- YS_AC_DTL_PKG.GET_AC_DTL_NM(D.AC_CODE_DTL,D.A_CODE,D.AC_DTL_TYP,:Lang_No) ,
ELSE
V_Name:=NULL;
Return(V_Name);
Exception When Others Then
Return (Null);
END GET_VER_CODE_NAME;

CREATE OR REPLACE TRIGGER IAS_JV_INSTALLMNT_Tgr
BEFORE DELETE OR INSERT OR UPDATE
OF A_CY
,J_AMT
,J_AMT_F
,AC_CODE_DTL
,BILL_NO
,BILL_SER
,I_NO
ON DETAIL_JOURNAL_V
REFERENCING NEW AS NEW OLD AS OLD
FOR EACH ROW
If DELETING Then --(1)
If :OLD.BILL_SER Is Not Null AND :OLD.I_NO Is Not Null AND :OLD.AC_CODE_DTL Is Not Null AND :OLD.AC_DTL_TYP=3 Then --(2)
UPDATE INSTALLMENT SET PAID_AMT=NVL(PAID_AMT,0)- DECODE(NVL(:OLD.J_AMT_F,0),0,ABS(:OLD.J_AMT),ABS(:OLD.J_AMT_F))
WHERE C_CODE=:OLD.AC_CODE_DTL
AND A_CY =:OLD.A_CY
AND BILL_SER =:OLD.BILL_SER
AND I_NO =:OLD.I_NO;
ELSIf :OLD.BILL_SER Is Not Null AND :OLD.I_NO Is Not Null AND :OLD.AC_CODE_DTL Is Not Null AND :OLD.AC_DTL_TYP=4 Then --(2)
UPDATE IAS_INSTALLMENT_PI SET PAID_AMT=NVL(PAID_AMT,0)- DECODE(NVL(:OLD.J_AMT_F,0),0,ABS(:OLD.J_AMT),ABS(:OLD.J_AMT_F))
WHERE V_CODE=:OLD.AC_CODE_DTL
End if;
ElsIf INSERTING Then --(1)
If :NEW.BILL_SER Is Not Null AND :NEW.I_NO Is Not Null AND :NEW.AC_CODE_DTL Is Not Null AND :NEW.AC_DTL_TYP=3 Then --(3)
UPDATE INSTALLMENT SET PAID_AMT=NVL(PAID_AMT,0) + DECODE(NVL(:NEW.J_AMT_F,0),0,ABS(:NEW.J_AMT),ABS(:NEW.J_AMT_F))
WHERE C_CODE=:NEW.AC_CODE_DTL
AND A_CY =:NEW.A_CY
AND BILL_SER =:NEW.BILL_SER
AND I_NO =:NEW.I_NO;
ELSIf :NEW.BILL_SER Is Not Null AND :NEW.I_NO Is Not Null AND :NEW.AC_CODE_DTL Is Not Null AND :NEW.AC_DTL_TYP=4 Then --(3)
UPDATE IAS_INSTALLMENT_PI SET PAID_AMT=NVL(PAID_AMT,0) + DECODE(NVL(:NEW.J_AMT_F,0),0,ABS(:NEW.J_AMT),ABS(:NEW.J_AMT_F))
WHERE V_CODE=:NEW.AC_CODE_DTL
End If;--(3)
ElsIf UPDATING Then --(1)
If :OLD.BILL_SER Is Not Null AND :OLD.I_NO Is Not Null AND :OLD.AC_CODE_DTL Is Not Null AND :OLD.AC_DTL_TYP=3 Then --(4)
UPDATE INSTALLMENT SET PAID_AMT=NVL(PAID_AMT,0)- DECODE (nvl(:OLD.J_AMT_F,0),0,ABS(nvl(:OLD.J_AMT,0)),ABS(nvl(:OLD.J_AMT_F,0)))
WHERE C_CODE =:OLD.AC_CODE_DTL
If :NEW.BILL_SER Is Not Null AND :NEW.I_NO Is Not Null AND :NEW.AC_CODE_DTL Is Not Null AND :NEW.AC_DTL_TYP=3 Then --(5)
UPDATE INSTALLMENT SET PAID_AMT=NVL(PAID_AMT,0) + DECODE(nvl(:NEW.J_AMT_F,0),0,ABS(nvl(:NEW.J_AMT,0)),ABS(nvl(:NEW.J_AMT_F,0)))
End if; --(5)
If :OLD.BILL_SER Is Not Null AND :OLD.I_NO Is Not Null AND :OLD.AC_CODE_DTL Is Not Null AND :OLD.AC_DTL_TYP=4 Then --(4)
UPDATE IAS_INSTALLMENT_PI SET PAID_AMT=NVL(PAID_AMT,0)- DECODE (nvl(:OLD.J_AMT_F,0),0,ABS(nvl(:OLD.J_AMT,0)),ABS(nvl(:OLD.J_AMT_F,0)))
WHERE V_CODE =:OLD.AC_CODE_DTL
If :NEW.BILL_SER Is Not Null AND :NEW.I_NO Is Not Null
AND :NEW.AC_CODE_DTL Is Not Null AND :NEW.AC_DTL_TYP=4 Then --(5)
UPDATE IAS_INSTALLMENT_PI SET PAID_AMT=NVL(PAID_AMT,0) + DECODE(nvl(:NEW.J_AMT_F,0),0,ABS(nvl(:NEW.J_AMT,0)),ABS(nvl(:NEW.J_AMT_F,0)))
End If;--(1)
EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN
Raise_Application_Error(-20303,' Error When Installment Trans. '||SqlErrm);
END;
CREATE OR REPLACE TRIGGER IAS_VD_INSTALLMNT_Tgr
,AC_AMT
,AC_AMTF
ON VOUCHER_DETAIL
If DELETING Then --(((1)
If :OLD.VOUCHER_TYPE = 1 AND :OLD.BILL_SER Is Not Null AND :OLD.I_NO Is Not Null AND :OLD.AC_CODE_DTL Is Not Null AND :OLD.AC_DTL_TYP=3 Then --(2)
UPDATE INSTALLMENT SET PAID_AMT=NVL(PAID_AMT,0)- DECODE(NVL(:OLD.AC_AMTF,0),0,ABS(:OLD.AC_AMT),ABS(:OLD.AC_AMTF))
ELSIf :OLD.VOUCHER_TYPE = 2 AND :OLD.BILL_SER Is Not Null AND :OLD.I_NO Is Not Null AND :OLD.AC_CODE_DTL Is Not Null AND :OLD.AC_DTL_TYP=4 Then --(2)
UPDATE IAS_INSTALLMENT_PI SET PAID_AMT=NVL(PAID_AMT,0)- DECODE(NVL(:OLD.AC_AMTF,0),0,ABS(:OLD.AC_AMT),ABS(:OLD.AC_AMTF))
If :NEW.VOUCHER_TYPE = 1 AND :NEW.BILL_SER Is Not Null AND :NEW.I_NO Is Not Null AND :NEW.AC_CODE_DTL Is Not Null AND :NEW.AC_DTL_TYP=3 Then --(3)
UPDATE INSTALLMENT SET PAID_AMT=NVL(PAID_AMT,0) + DECODE(NVL(:NEW.AC_AMTF,0),0,ABS(:NEW.AC_AMT),ABS(:NEW.AC_AMTF))
ELSIf :NEW.VOUCHER_TYPE = 2 AND :NEW.BILL_SER Is Not Null AND :NEW.I_NO Is Not Null AND :NEW.AC_CODE_DTL Is Not Null AND :NEW.AC_DTL_TYP=4 Then --(3)
UPDATE IAS_INSTALLMENT_PI SET PAID_AMT=NVL(PAID_AMT,0) + DECODE(NVL(:NEW.AC_AMTF,0),0,ABS(:NEW.AC_AMT),ABS(:NEW.AC_AMTF))
If :OLD.VOUCHER_TYPE = 1 AND :OLD.BILL_SER Is Not Null AND :OLD.I_NO Is Not Null AND :OLD.AC_CODE_DTL Is Not Null AND :OLD.AC_DTL_TYP=3 Then
UPDATE INSTALLMENT SET PAID_AMT=NVL(PAID_AMT,0)- DECODE(nvl(:OLD.AC_AMTF,0),0,ABS(nvl(:OLD.AC_AMT,0)),ABS(nvl(:OLD.AC_AMTF,0)))
If :NEW.VOUCHER_TYPE = 1 AND :NEW.BILL_SER Is Not Null AND :NEW.I_NO Is Not Null AND :NEW.AC_CODE_DTL Is Not Null AND :NEW.AC_DTL_TYP=3 Then
UPDATE INSTALLMENT SET PAID_AMT=NVL(PAID_AMT,0) + DECODE(nvl(:NEW.AC_AMTF,0),0,ABS(nvl(:NEW.AC_AMT,0)),ABS(nvl(:NEW.AC_AMTF,0)))
If :OLD.VOUCHER_TYPE = 2 AND :OLD.BILL_SER Is Not Null AND :OLD.I_NO Is Not Null AND :OLD.AC_CODE_DTL Is Not Null AND :OLD.AC_DTL_TYP=4 Then
UPDATE IAS_INSTALLMENT_PI SET PAID_AMT=NVL(PAID_AMT,0)- DECODE (nvl(:OLD.AC_AMTF,0),0,ABS(nvl(:OLD.AC_AMT,0)),ABS(nvl(:OLD.AC_AMTF,0)))
If :NEW.VOUCHER_TYPE = 2 AND :NEW.BILL_SER Is Not Null AND :NEW.I_NO Is Not Null AND :NEW.AC_CODE_DTL Is Not Null AND :NEW.AC_DTL_TYP=4 Then
UPDATE IAS_INSTALLMENT_PI SET PAID_AMT=NVL(PAID_AMT,0) + DECODE(nvl(:NEW.AC_AMTF,0),0,ABS(nvl(:NEW.AC_AMT,0)),ABS(nvl(:NEW.AC_AMTF,0)))
EPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN
Teriggers Created
:=NAME_IN('Parameter.BRN_USR');
v_Scr_Typ Number(10) :=NAME_IN('Parameter.Scr_Typ');
v_Brn_Year Number(4) :=NAME_IN('Parameter.BRN_YEAR');
v_Blk Varchar2(50):=NAME_IN('Parameter.BLK');
--v_Rep_Ext Varchar2(5) :=NAME_IN('Parameter.REP_EXT');
v_Rep_Lng Varchar2(5);
v_Lng_Dir Number:=1;
v_form_no Number:=Name_In('PARAMETER.FORM_NO');

v_scr_orgnl Number:=Name_In('PARAMETER.SCR_ORGNL');

V_Trmnl_Nm Varchar2(100):=NAME_IN('PARAMETER.Trmnl_Nm');
v_Use_Rsltn_Scr Number:=1;

V_Use_Unicode Number:=Name_In('PARAMETER.USE_UNICODE');

Create Or Replace Force View Ias_V_Chk_CstTrns as
Select 1 doc_type,D.ac_code_dtl c_code,a_cy a_cy,D.j_doc_no doc_no,D.jv_type jv_type ,
M.j_ser doc_ser,D.cc_code cc_code , D.rcrd_no
From master_journal_v M,detail_journal_v D ,Customer C
where M.j_ser = D.j_ser
and D.a_code = C.c_a_code
and D.ac_code_dtl = C.c_code
and D.ac_dtl_typ=3
and nvl(stand_by,0) = 0
Minus
Select doc_type,ac_code_dtl c_code,a_cy,doc_no doc_no,jv_type jv_type,
doc_ser,cc_code,rcrd_no
From Ias_Post_dtl
where doc_type=1
and ac_dtl_typ=3
Create Or Replace Force View Ias_V_Chk_VndTrns as
Select 1 doc_type,D.AC_CODE_DTL v_code,a_cy,D.j_doc_no ,D.jv_type jv_type ,
M.j_ser doc_ser,D.cc_code
From Master_journal_v M,Detail_journal_v D ,V_details C
and D.a_code = C.v_a_code
and D.AC_CODE_DTL = C.v_code
and D.AC_CODE_DTL Is Not Null
AND D.AC_DTL
_TYP=4
Select doc_type,AC_CODE_DTL v_code,a_cy,doc_no ,jv_type jv_type,
doc_ser,cc_code
From Ias_Post_Dtl
and AC_CODE_DTL Is Not Null
AND AC_DTL_TYP=4

--##_____________________________________________________________________________________________________##--
PROCEDURE GET_PARA ( P_LANG_NO IN PLS_INTEGER DEFAULT 1,
P_USER_NO IN PLS_INTEGER ,
P_POST_TYPE IN NUMBER DEFAULT 0) IS
V_LANG_NO:=P_LANG_NO;
V_USER_NO:=P_USER_NO;
SELECT CUR_CODE
INTO V_LOCAL_CUR
FROM EX_RATE
WHERE L_F=1;
RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(-20601,IAS_GEN_PKG.GET_MSG(V_LANG_NO,227)||SQLERRM) ;
SELECT NVL(VAT_PER,0),NVL(POST_REP_COMM,0),NVL(CALC_VAT_AMT_TYPE,0),NVL(USE_VAT,0)
INTO V_VAT_PER,V_POST_REP_COMM,V_CALC_VAT_AMT_TYPE,V_USE_VAT
FROM SHP_PARA,IAS_PARA_GEN;
EXCEPTION
WHEN OTHERS THEN
RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(-20603,IAS_GEN_PKG.GET_MSG(V_LANG_NO,670)||SQLERRM) ;
--=======================================================================
END GET_PARA;
) Is
SELECT SHP_SLS_AC,SHP_DISC_AC,SHP_OTHR_AC,SHP_VAT_AC
INTO V_SLS_AC,V_DISC_AC,V_OTHR_AC,V_VAT_AC
From Interface_Acc
Where Brn_No=P_Brn_No
When No_Data_Found Then
Raise_application_error(-20606,Ias_Gen_Pkg.Get_Msg(V_Lang_No,1094)||SqlErrm) ;
End Get_Interface_Ac;

,P_POST_TYPE IN NUMBER DEFAULT 0) IS
V_amt_loc NUMBER;
V_amt_F NUMBER;

(SELECT
95 DOC_TYPE
,Mv.DOC_NO DOC_NO
,Mv.BILL_DOC_TYPE JV_TYPE
,Mv.DOC_SER
DOC_SER
,Mv.DOC_DATE DOC_DATE
,Mv.CASH_NO CASH_NO
,DECODE(Mv.BILL_DOC_TYPE,4,Mv.C_CODE) C_CODE
,Mv.A_CODE A_CODE
,NVL(Mv.BILL_RATE,1) AC_RATE
,Mv.REP_CODE REP_CODE
,Mv.A_CY A_CY
,Mv.COMM_PER COMM_PER
,Mv.SHP_TYPE BILL_TYPE
,Mv.REF_NO REF_NO
,Mv.A_DSC DOC_DESC
,Mv.EVRY_ADD_WGHT_PRICE EVRY_ADD_WGHT_PRICE
,NVL(Mv.BILL_AMT,0)*NVL(Mv.BILL_RATE,1) BILL_AMT
,DECODE(Mv.A_CY,V_LOCAL_CUR,0,NVL(Mv.BILL_AMT,0)) BILL_AMTF
,(NVL(DISC_AMT,0)*NVL(Mv.BILL_RATE,1)) DISC_AMT
,DECODE(Mv.A_CY,V_LOCAL_CUR,0,(NVL(DISC_AMT,0))) DISC_AMTF
,Mv.OTHR_AMT *NVL(Mv.BILL_RATE,1) OTHR_AMT
,DECODE(Mv.A_CY,V_LOCAL_CUR,0,(NVL(OTHR_AMT,0))) OTHR_AMTF
,Mv.VAT_AMT *NVL(Mv.BILL_RATE,1) VAT_AMT
,DECODE(Mv.A_CY,V_LOCAL_CUR,0,(NVL(VAT_AMT,0))) VAT_AMTF
,NVL(Mv.CHEQUE_AMT,0) *NVL(Mv.BILL_RATE,1) CHEQUE_AMT
,DECODE(Mv.A_CY,V_LOCAL_CUR,0,NVL(Mv.CHEQUE_AMT,0)) CHEQUE_AMTF
,((NVL(Mv.BILL_AMT,0)-(NVL(DISC_AMT,0)))* NVL(Mv.COMM_PER,0))/100 COMM_AMT
,DECODE(Mv.A_CY,V_LOCAL_CUR,0,((NVL(Mv.BILL_AMT,0)
-(NVL(DISC_AMT,0))) * NVL(Mv.COMM_PER,0))/100) COMM_AMTF
,Mv.CHEQUE_NO CHEQUE_NO
,Mv.CC_CODE CC_CODE
,Mv.PJ_NO PJ_NO
,Mv.ACTV_NO ACTV_NO
,Mv.CASH_AC_FCC CASH_AC_FCC
,Mv.CASH_NO_FCC CASH_NO_FCC
,BILL_DUE_DATE DOC_DUE_DATE
,Mv.SHP_CNT SHP_CNT
,R_CODE R_CODE
,Mv.DOC_BRN_NO DOC_BRN_NO
,AUDIT_REF_DESC AUDIT_REF_DESC
,AUDIT_REF_U_ID AUDIT_REF_U_ID
,AUDIT_REF_DATE AUDIT_REF_DATE
,UNPOST_U_ID UNPOST_U_ID
,UNPOST_DATE UNPOST_DATE
,AD_DATE AD_DATE
,Mv.AD_U_ID
AD_U_ID
,UP_DATE UP_DATE
,UP_U_ID UP_U_ID
,UP_CNT UP_CNT
,Mv.CMP_NO CMP_NO
,Mv.BRN_NO BRN_NO
,Mv.BRN_YEAR BRN_YEAR
,Mv.BRN_USR BRN_USR
,AUDIT_REF AUDIT_REF
,BILL_PY MOV_DOC_PY
FROM
SHP_PREPAID_SLS_MST Mv
WHERE
NVL(Mv.SHP_POST,0)=DECODE(NVL(P_POST_TYPE,0),0,0,NVL(Mv.SHP_POST,0))

AND NVL(Mv.BILL_PY,0)=0

AND Mv.DOC_DATE BETWEEN NVL(P_F_DOC_DATE,Mv.DOC_DATE) AND NVL(P_T_DOC_DATE,Mv.DOC_DATE)
And Mv.DOC_SER =NVL(P_DOC_SER, Mv.DOC_SER)

AND Mv.AD_U_ID = NVL(P_AD_U_ID, Mv.AD_U_ID) And EXISTS
(SELECT 1 FROM S_BRN_USR_PRIV
WHERE U_ID = V_USER_NO
AND S_BRN_USR_PRIV.BRN_NO = Mv.BRN_NO
AND NVL(ADD_FLAG,1) = 1
AND ROWNUM <= 1
FOR UPDATE NOWAIT)
LOOP --(1)
IF NVL(IAS_POSTING_PKG.CHK_EXIST_PST_DOC(M_CV.DOC_TYPE,M_CV.DOC_SER) ,0) = 0 THEN -- (BEGIN CHK EXIST) --

/* If M_cv.a_cy <> v_local_cur Then

-- DR SLS_AC

"PKG INIT"IAS_UNPOSTTRNS_PRC""
CREATE OR REPLACE PROCEDURE IAS_UnpostTrns_Prc ( P_User_no In Number,
P_DocType In Out Number,
P_Wh_MJSql_Mst In varchar2,
P_Wh_MJSql_Dtl In varchar2,
P_Wh_DocSql In varchar2,
P_Wh_DocSql_Dtl In varchar2,
P_Del_Or_Not In Number DEFAULT 0,
P_Close_Per In Out NUMBER) Is
Cnt Number;
CLMN_DOC_NO
VarChar2(50);
CLMN_DOC_DATE
CLMN_JV_TYPE
CLMN_Doc_Ser
CLMN_Doc_Post
CLMN_EXTRNL_POST VarChar2(50);
TABLE_NAME
--##-------------------------------------
--Mrd Updated in 18/0/2016 check glslib
Ias_Report_Pkg.Get_Clmn_Name(
P_DocType
,--## Get Table Name and Column Name Use In Update Post Flag in Tables
TABLE_NAME );
--## Check EXISTS EXTERNAL_POST --
Execute Immediate 'Select 1 From USER_TAB_COLUMNS Where TABLE_NAME='''||TABLE_NAME||'''
AND COLUMN_NAME= ''EXTERNAL_POST''
and RowNum<=1 ' Into cnt;
Exception When no_data_found then
cnt:=0;
Raise_application_error(-20204,'There Is Error when Select Ias_Post_Mst ,'||SqlErrm) ;
If NVL(cnt,0) > 0 Then --(1)
CLMN_EXTRNL_POST:=' AND NVL(EXTERNAL_POST,0)=0';
/* If TABLE_NAME NOT IN('IAS_PR_BILL_MST','STK_ADJUSTMENT','ASSEMBLE_KIT_ITEMS','DISASSEMBLE_KIT_ITEMS',
'NOTE','SHP_BILLING','SHP_PREPAID_SLS_MST','REM_CNTRCT_RNT_MST') THEN
End If; */
--## Check Closing Periods --
Execute Immediate 'Select 1 From Ias_Post_Mst Where EXISTS ( SELECT 1 FROM S_PRD_BRN_CLS WHERE PRD_NO = Ias_Post_Mst.MNTH AND BRN_NO = Ias_Post_Mst.BRN_NO AND PRD_CLS = 1 AND ROWNUM<=1) '|| ' And '||P_Wh_MJSql_Mst||' and RowNum<=1 ' Into cnt;
If cnt > 0 Then --(1)
P_Close_Per:=1;
Else -- (1)
If Nvl(P_Del_Or_Not,0) = 1 Then--## Delete From Ias_Post_Mst and Ias_Post_Dtl (Cascade)
If P_DocType Not In(83,84) Then
Execute Immediate
' Delete From Ias_Post_Mst
Where EXISTS ( SELECT 1 FROM S_PRD_BRN_CLS WHERE PRD_NO =Ias_Post_Mst.MNTH AND BRN_NO = Ias_Post_Mst.BRN_NO AND PRD_CLS = 0 AND ROWNUM<=1)
and nvl(EXTERNAL_POST,0)= 0
--and nvl(DOC_POST,0)= 1
and Exists (select 1 From S_Brn_Usr_Priv
Where U_Id = '||p_User_No||'
And S_Brn_Usr_Priv.Brn_No =Ias_Post_Mst.Brn_No
And Nvl(Add_Flag,1) = 1
And RowNum <=1 ) and '||P_Wh_MJSql_Mst||'';
' Delete From Ias_Post_Dtl
Where EXISTS ( SELECT 1 FROM S_PRD_BRN_CLS WHERE PRD_NO =Ias_Post_Dtl.MNTH AND BRN_NO = Ias_Post_Dtl.BRN_NO AND PRD_CLS = 0 AND ROWNUM<=1)
-- and nvl(DOC_POST,0)= 1
and Doc_Sequence Is Not Null
And Exists (select 1 From S_Brn_Usr_Priv
And S_Brn_Usr_Priv.Brn_No =Ias_Post_dtl.Brn_No
If P_DocType Not In ( 83 , 84 ) Then -- Cheque Due --
' Update Ias_Post_Mst Set Doc_post=0 ,Unpost_date=Sysdate ,Unpost_u_id='||P_User_no||'
' Update Ias_Post_Dtl Set Doc_post=0 ,Unpost_date=Sysdate ,Unpost_u_id='||P_User_no||'
And nvl(EXTERNAL_POST,0)= 0
And S_Brn_Usr_Priv.Brn_No =Ias_Post_Dtl.Brn_No
And RowNum <=1 ) and '||P_Wh_MJSql_Dtl||'';
--------------------------------------------------------------
If P_DocType Is Not Null Then --(2)
--## Update Post Flag = 0 --
-- insert into tst values(P_Wh_DocSql);
' Update '||TABLE_NAME||' a Set '||Clmn_Doc_Post||'=0 ,Unpost_date=Sysdate ,Unpost_u_id='||P_User_no||' Where
Exists (select 1 From S_Brn_Usr_Priv
And S_Brn_Usr_Priv.Brn_No =a.Brn_No
And RowNum <=1 )
and '||P_Wh_DocSql||' '||CLMN_EXTRNL_POST||'';
-- and Exists(select doc_ser from ias_post_dtl where doc_close=0 and doc_ser=a.'||CLMN_Doc_Ser||' )
If P_DocType = 775 Then -- Salary Calc
' Update HRS_SLRY_CALC_MST_HSTRY a Set DOC_POST =0 ,Unpost_date=Sysdate ,Unpost_u_id='||P_User_no||' Where
Exists (Select 1 From S_Brn_Usr_Priv
And RowNum <=1 ) and '||P_Wh_DocSql||'';
ElsIf P_DocType In ( 2,3 ,83,84) Then -- Receipt / Payment
' Update Voucher_Detail Set cheque_post=0 ,Unpost_date=Sysdate ,Unpost_u_id='||P_User_no||' Where Exists (select 1 From S_Brn_Usr_Priv
And S_Brn_Usr_Priv.Brn_No =Voucher_Detail.Brn_No
And RowNum <=1 ) and '||P_Wh_DocSql_Dtl||'';
ElsIf P_DocType =6 Then
' Update gr_note Set GR_POST=0 ,Unpost_date=Sysdate ,Unpost_u_id='||P_User_no||' Where Exists (select 1 From S_Brn_Usr_Priv
And S_Brn_Usr_Priv.Brn_No =gr_note.Brn_No
Raise_application_error(-20205,'Error When Updating Document Doc_type ='||P_DocType||' '||SqlErrm) ;
End If; --(2)
--==========================================================================
Exception When others then
Raise_application_error(-20206,'Error When Unposting Document In Ias_Post_Mst = '||SqlErrm) ;
End IAS_UnpostTrns_Prc;

CREATE OR REPLACE PACKAGE Body Ias_Report_Pkg AS
Function Get_Rep_Para ( P_SUM_DTL In Number Default Null, -- 0 - Detail , 1 - Summery --
P_Doc_TYPE In Number Default Null,
P_Jv_TYPE In Number Default Null,
P_Doc_No In Number Default Null,
P_Doc_Ser In Number Default Null,
USER_NO In Number ,
v_show_hdr In Number ,
Brn_No In Number ,
F_SYS_NO In Number ,
LANG_NO In Number Default 1 ,
P_DIR In Number Default 1 ,
P_FTR In VarChar2 Default Null,
P_HDR In VarChar2 Default Null,
P_VAR In VarChar2 Default Null,
P_NUM In Number Default Null,
P_Gen_Inv In Number Default 1 ,-- P_Gen_Inv := 1 - General Doc Type , 2 - Inv Doc Type --
P_Weher In VarChar2 Default Null,-- Condition Addition --
P_REP_NM In VarChar2 Default Null,
P_Tbl_Nm_Mst In Varchar2 Default Null,
P_Tbl_Nm_Dtl In Varchar2 Default Null) RETURN VarChar2 Is
Select Nvl(ALLOW_CALL_REP_BTN,0) ,Nvl(HIDE_COST,0)
Into CALL_BTN,HID_COST From PRIVILEGE_FIXED
Where U_ID=USER_NO;
CALL_BTN :=1;
END ;
If Nvl(CALL_BTN ,0)=0 Then
Return('NOT');
G_Doc_TYPE := P_Doc_TYPE;
G_Jv_TYPE := P_Doc_TYPE;
G_Doc_No := P_Doc_No;
G_Doc_Ser := P_Doc_Ser;
G_Usr_No := User_No;
G_Brn_No := Brn_No;
G_Lang_No := Lang_No;
Null;
If Nvl(P_Gen_Inv,1)=2 Then
Select Decode(P_DOC_TYPE,
0 ,0 ,
1 ,4 ,
2 ,6 ,
3 ,5 ,
4 ,7,
5 ,8,
6 ,9,
7 ,11,
8 ,12,
11,17,
12,17,
13,18,
14,18,
15,10,
16,10,
17,10,
18,10) Into G_DOC_TYPE From Dual;
G_DOC_TYPE :=P_DOC_TYPE;
G_Usr_Nm:= ias_gen_pkg.get_prompt(Lang_no,2524)||' : '||IAS_USR_PKG.Get_Usr_Nm(G_Usr_No,Lang_No);
G_Usr_Nm:
= G_Usr_No;
G_REP_DAT := Ias_Gen_Pkg.Get_Prompt(Lang_No,2489)||' : '||Ias_Gen_Pkg.Get_Curdate();
If G_Doc_Ser Is Not Null Then -- P_REP_NM --
If P_Gen_Inv = 2 Then
Get_Priv_Rep_Name_Inv ;
Get_Priv_Rep_Name ;
End If ;
HDR:=Doc_Type_Name(Lang_No,P_Doc_TYPE,P_Jv_TYPE);
If Lang_No<>1 Then
Select
substr(V_REP_NM,1,instr(V_REP_NM,' ')-1)||'_F'||
substr(V_REP_NM,instr(V_REP_NM,' '), Length(V_REP_NM)) Into V_REP_NM
From dual;

If LANG_NO <> 1 Then
V_Rep_Nm:=V_Rep_Nm||'_F';
End If; -- P_REP_NM --
If v_show_hdr = 1 Then
Get_Rep_Head;
CmpANAME:=Null;
CmpENAME:=Null;
BANAME :=Null;
BENAME :=Null;
BADD :=Null;
BEADD :=Null;
BTELE :=Null;
BFAX :=Null;
BBOX :=Null;
BETELE :=Null;
BEFAX :=Null;
BEBOX :=Null;
BIMG :=Null;
Str:=
' B_TELE="'||BTELE||'"'||'
B_FAX="'||BFAX||'"'||'
B_BOX="'||BBOX||'"'||'
B_ADD="'||BADD||'"'||'

ElsIf V_REP_NM Is Not Null Then
Return(V_REP_NM||'
'||P_Weher||'
'||STR);
End Get_Rep_Para;

PROCEDURE Get_Rep_Head Is
v_Head_Type Number;
Select nvl(Head_type,1) Into v_Head_Type
From Ias_Para_Gen;
v_Head_Type:=1;
If v_Head_Type = 1 Then
Select Decode(G_Lang_no,1,CMP_LNAME,CMP_FNAME),
Decode(G_Lang_no,1,CMP_FNAME,CMP_LNAME),
Decode(G_Lang_no,1,BRN_LDES,BRN_FDES),
Decode(G_Lang_no,1,BRN_FDES,BRN_LDES),
Decode(G_Lang_no,1,BRN_LAdd,BRN_FAdd),
Decode(G_Lang_no,1,BRN_FAdd,BRN_LAdd),
Decode(G_Lang_no,1,BRN_LTele,BRN_FTele),
Decode(G_Lang_no,1,BRN_FTele,BRN_LTele),
Decode(G_Lang_no,1,BRN_LFax,BRN_FFax),
Decode(G_Lang_no,1,BRN_FFax,BRN_LFax),
Decode(G_Lang_no,1,BRN_LBox,BRN_FFax),
Decode(G_Lang_no,1,BRN_FBox,BRN_LBox),
CMP_IMG
Into CmpAName ,
CmpEName ,
BAName ,
BEName ,
BAdd ,
BEAdd ,
BTele ,
BETele ,
BFax ,
BEFax ,
BBox ,
BEBox ,
BIMG
From S_Brn
Where Brn_No=G_BRN_No;
null;
Select Decode(G_Lang_no,1,CMP_ANAME,CMP_ENAME),
Decode(G_Lang_no,1,CMP_ENAME,CMP_ANAME),
Decode(G_Lang_no,1,BRN_ADES,BRN_EDES),
Decode(G_Lang_no,1,BRN_EDES,BRN_ADES),
Decode(G_Lang_no,1,BRN_AAdd,BRN_EAdd),
Decode(G_Lang_no,1,BRN_EAdd,BRN_AAdd),
Decode(G_Lang_no,1,BRN_ATele,BRN_ETele),
Decode(G_Lang_no,1,BRN_ETele,BRN_ATele),
Decode(G_Lang_no,1,BRN_AFax,BRN_EFax),
Decode(G_Lang_no,1,BRN_EFax,BRN_AFax),
Decode(G_Lang_no,1,BRN_ABox,BRN_EFax),
Decode(G_Lang_no,1,BRN_EBox,BRN_ABox),
From IAS_USER_HEAD
Where U_id=G_Usr_No;
Decode(G_Lang_no,1,BR
N_LBox,BRN_FFax),
Where Brn_No=G_BRN_No And RowNum<=1;
CmpANAME:=replace(CmpANAME,'','');
CmpENAME:=replace(CmpENAME,'','');
BANAME :=replace(BANAME,'','');
BENAME :=replace(BENAME,'','');
BADD :=replace(BADD,'','');
BEADD :=replace(BEADD,'','');
BTELE :=replace(BTELE,'','');
BFAX :=replace(BFAX,'','');
BBOX :=replace(BBOX,'','');
BETELE :=replace(BETELE,'','');
BEFAX :=replace(BEFAX,'','');
BEBOX :=replace(BEBOX,'','');
BIMG :=replace(BIMG,'','');
End Get_Rep_Head ;
Function Get_Tbl_Nm_Mst ( P_Doc_Type In Number ) Return Varchar2 Is
V_Tbl_Nm_Mst Varchar2(100) ;
If P_Doc_Type = 1 Then
V_Tbl_Nm_Mst :='Master_Journal_V' ;
ElsIf P_Doc_Type = 2 Then
V_Tbl_Nm_Mst :='Vouchers' ;
ElsIf P_Doc_Type = 3 Then
ElsIf P_Doc_Type = 4 Then
V_Tbl_Nm_Mst :='Ias_Bill_Mst' ;
ElsIf P_Doc_Type = 5 Then
V_Tbl_Nm_Mst :='Ias_Rt_Bill_Mst' ;
ElsIf P_Doc_Type = 6 Then
V_Tbl_Nm_Mst :='Ias_Pi_Bill_Mst' ;
ElsIf P_Doc_Type = 7 Then
V_Tbl_Nm_Mst :='Ias_Pr_Bill_Mst' ;

V_Tbl_Nm_Mst :='Gr_Note' ;

V_Tbl_Nm_Mst :='Ias_Outgoing_Mst' ;

V_Tbl_Nm_Mst :='Stk_Adjustment' ;
ElsIf P_Doc_Type = 11 Then
V_Tbl_Nm_Mst :='Ias_Whtrns_Mst' ;
ElsIf P_Doc_Type = 12 Then
ElsIf P_Doc_Type = 13 Then
V_Tbl_Nm_Mst :='Master_Out_Bills' ;

V_Tbl_Nm_Mst :='ias_pi_bill_mst_add_disc' ;

ElsIf P_Doc_Type = 300 The

ElsIf P_Doc_Type = 301 Then
V_Tbl_Nm_Mst :='PMS_SUB_CONTRACTS_MST' ;
ElsIf P_Doc_Type = 302 Then
V_Tbl_Nm_Mst :='PMS_FINCE_RECEIPT_MST' ;
ElsIf P_Doc_Type = 303 Then
V_Tbl_Nm_Mst :='PMS_S_FINCE_RECEIPT_MST' ;

ElsIf P_Doc_Type = 502 Then
V_Tbl_Nm_Mst :='REM_CNTRCT_RNT_CNCL_MST' ;

Function Get_Tbl_Nm_Dtl ( P_Doc_Type In Number ) Return Varchar2 Is
V_Tbl_Nm_Dtl Varchar2(100) ;
V_Tbl_Nm_Dtl :='Detail_Journal_V' ;

V_Tbl_Nm_Dtl :='Ias_Pr_Bill_Dtl' ;
V_Tbl_Nm_Dtl :='Gr_Detail' ;
V_Tbl_Nm_Dtl :='Ias_Outgoing_Dtl' ;
V_Tbl_Nm_Dtl :='Stk_Adjustment_Det' ;
V_Tbl_Nm_Dtl :='Ias_Whtrns_Dtl' ;
V_Tbl_Nm_Dtl :='Detail_Out_Bills' ;
V_Tbl_Nm_Dtl :='Ias_Ret_Bill_Dtl' ;
V_Tbl_Nm_Dtl :='IAS_BILL_DTL_ADD_DISC' ;

Function Get_Clmn_Labl( P_TBLFLD_NAME In VarChar2 ,
LANG_NO In Number Default 1 ) RETURN VarChar2 Is
Cment VarChar2(100);
Tabl VarChar2(100):=Upper(Substr(P_TBLFLD_NAME,1,Instr(P_TBLFLD_NAME,'.')-1));
Clmn VarChar2(100):=Upper(Substr(P_TBLFLD_NAME,Instr(P_TBLFLD_NAME,'.',-1)+1,Length(P_TBLFLD_NAME)));
To_Char(Case When To_Number(Ascii(Substr(Comments,1,1))) Between 49 And 57 Then
Ias_Gen_Pkg.Get_Prompt(Lang_No,To_Number(Comments)) Else Comments End)
Into Cment From User_Col_Comments
Where Table_Name ||'.'|| Column_Name = Nvl(Tabl,Table_Name)||'.'|| Clmn;
Where Table_Name ||'.'|| Column_Name = Nvl(Tabl,Table_Name)||'.'|| Clmn
And To_Number(Ascii(Substr(Comments,1,1))) Between 49 And 57;
Select Comments
Cment:= P_TBLFLD_NAME;
Return(Cment);
End Get_Clmn_Labl ;
Function Get_Clmn_Type( P_TBLFLD_NAME In VarChar2 ,
P_LENGTH In Out Number ) RETURN VarChar2 Is
TypeD VarChar2(20);
Lngth Number;
Select Data_Type , Data_Length
Into TypeD , P_LENGTH
From USER
_TAB_COLS
Where TABLE_NAME||'.'||COLUMN_NAME = Nvl(Tabl,Table_Name)||'.'|| Clmn
And Table_Name In (Select Table_Name From User_Tab_Comments Where Table_Type='TABLE') ;
From USER_TAB_COLS
And Table_Name In (Select Table_Name From User_Tab_Comments Where Table_Type='TABLE')
Select Data_Type
Into TypeD
TypeD:='VARCHAR2';
Return(TypeD);
End Get_Clmn_Type;
End Ias_Report_Pkg;
_Prompt(P_Lng_no,TO_NUMBER(SUBSTR(Comments,1,4)))

"PKG INIT"IAS_POST_LIST_PKG""
CREATE OR REPLACE PACKAGE IAS_Post_List_Pkg AS
TYPE Post_List_Rec IS REF CURSOR ;
PROCEDURE Post_List_View ( List_Qry IN OUT Post_List_Rec ,
P_date IN Date,
P_Jv_Type IN Number,
P_Cash_Bank IN Cash_IN_Hand.Cash_No%Type,
P_W_Code IN WareHouse_Details.W_Code%Type,
P_CS IN Cost_Centers.cc_code%Type,
P_User_No IN Number,
P_Post_Jv_Type IN Number,
P_Pur_type IN Number Default 0,
P_Order_By IN Varchar2,
P_Brn_No IN Number Default 0,
P_Ad_U_Id IN Number ,
P_POST_UNPOST IN NUMBER);
Procedure UnPost_List_Inv_View ( List_Qry In Out Post_List_Rec ,
P_Date In Date ,
P_Jv_Type In Number ,
P_Cash_Bank In Cash_In_Hand.Cash_No%Type ,
P_W_Code In WareHouse_Details.W_Code%Type,
P_Cs In Cost_Centers.cc_code%Type ,
P_User_No In Number ,
P_UnPost_Jv_Type In Number ,
P_Order_By In Varchar2 ) ;
END IAS_Post_List_Pkg;
CREATE OR REPLACE PACKAGE BODY IAS_Post_List_Pkg AS
-- - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - Posting List View - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - -
PROCEDURE Post_List_View (
List_Qry IN OUT Post_List_Rec ,
P_Cash_Bank IN Cash_In_Hand.Cash_No%Type,
P_Ad_U_Id IN Number,
P_POST_UNPOST IN NUMBER) IS --40 POST 104 UNPOST
v_Use_Audit_Doc Number(1);
--## Get Parameters
Select nvl(Use_Audit_Doc,0)
Into v_Use_Audit_Doc
v_Use_Audit_Doc:=0 ;
-- - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - Journal_V - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - -
If P_Post_Jv_Type = 1 Then
Open List_Qry For
'Select J_Ser,
jv_type,
j_doc_no,
j_date,
0 Cash_No,
0 W_Code,
0 Cs,
0 Cheque_No,
0 Ac_Code,
0 DOC_SEQUENCE,
0 GR_NO,
0 G_Ser
From Master_Journal_V
Where J_Date = Nvl(:P_Date , J_date)
And Nvl(Jv_Type,0) = Decode(:P_Jv_Type,0,Nvl(Jv_Type,0),:P_Jv_Type)
And nvl(Audit_ref,0) = Decode(:v_Use_Audit_Doc,1,1,nvl(Audit_ref,0))
And Nvl(ad_u_id,0) = Decode(:P_ad_u_id,0,Nvl(ad_u_id,0),:P_ad_u_id)
And Brn_No = Master_Journal_V.Brn_No
And RowNum <= 1 )
AND (1=:P_User_No
OR EXISTS (SELECT JV_TYPE
FROM JV_TYPES_DETAIL
WHERE JV_TYPE = Master_Journal_V.JV_TYPE
AND U_ID = :P_User_No
AND Nvl(VIEW_FLAG,0) = 1 ) )
And Nvl(J_Post,0)=Decode(:P_POST_UNPOST,1,0,1)
And (Nvl(J_Post,0)=0 OR Exists ( Select Doc_Ser From Ias_Post_Mst
Where Doc_Close=0
and Doc_Post = 1
and Doc_type = 1
and Doc_Ser=Master_Journal_V.J_Ser
and RowNum<=1))
And Nvl(Stand_By,0)=0 '||P_Order_By
Using
P_date,
P_Jv_Type,
v_Use_Audit_Doc,
P_Brn_No,
P_ad_u_id,
P_User_No,
P_POST_UNPOST ;
-- - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - Receipt - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - -
ElsIf P_Post_Jv_Type = 2 Then
'Select V_Ser
J_Ser,
Voucher_Pay_Type Jv_type,
Voucher_No J_doc_no,
Voucher_Date J_date,
Cash_No,
From Vouchers
Where Voucher_Type = 1
And Voucher_Date = Nvl(:P_Date , Voucher_Date)
And Nvl(Voucher_Pay_Type,0) =Decode(:P_Jv_Type,0,Nvl(Voucher_Pay_Type,0),:P_Jv_Type)
And Nvl(Cash_No,0) =Decode(Nvl(:P_Cash_Bank,0) ,0,Nvl(Cash_No,0),Nvl(:P_Cash_Bank,0) )
And Nvl(Audit_ref,0) = Decode(:v_Use_Audit_Doc,1,1,nvl(Audit_ref,0))
And Nvl(Move_Cheq_Py,0)=0
And Brn_No = Vouchers.Brn_No
And Nvl(Add_Flag,1) = 1 And RowNum <= 1 )
And Nvl(Voucher_Post,0)=Decode(:P_POST_UNPOST,1,0,1)
And (Nvl(Voucher_Post,0)=0 OR Exists ( Select Doc_Ser From Ias_Post_Mst
and Doc_type = 2
and Doc_Ser=Vouchers.V_Ser
P_Cash_Bank ,
-- - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - Payment - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - -
ElsIf P_Post_Jv_Type = 3 Then
'Select V_Ser J_Ser,
Voucher_No
J_doc_no,
Where Voucher_Type = 2
And Nvl(Voucher_Pay_Type,0) = Decode(:P_Jv_Type,0,Nvl(Voucher_Pay_Type,0),:P_Jv_Type)
and Doc_type = 3
Using P_date,
P_POST_UNPOST;
-- - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - Bills - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - -
ElsIf P_Post_Jv_Type = 4 Then
'Select Bill_Ser J_Ser,
Bill_Doc_Type Jv_type,
Bill_No J_doc_no,
Bill_Date J_date,
W_Code,
From Ias_Bill_Mst
Where Bill_Date = Nvl(:P_Date , Bill_Date)
And Nvl(Bill_Doc_Type,0) =Decode(:P_Jv_Type,0,Nvl(Bill_Doc_Type,0),:P_Jv_Type)
And Nvl(cc_code,''0'') =Decode(:P_Cs,''0'',Nvl(cc_code,''0''),:P_Cs)
And Nvl(w_code,0) =Decode(nvl(:P_W_Code,0),0,Nvl(w_code,0),nvl(:P_W_Code,0))
And Brn_No = Ias_Bill_Mst.Brn_No
And Nvl(Ias_Bill_Mst.Bill_Py,0)=0
And Nvl(Bill_Post,0)=Decode(:P_POST_UNPOST,1,0,1)
And (Nvl(Bill_Post,0)=0 OR Exists ( Select Doc_Ser From Ias_Post_Mst
and Doc_type = 4
and Doc_Ser=Ias_Bill_Mst.Bill_Ser
P_Cs,
P_W_Code,
-- - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - Rt Bills - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - -
ElsIf P_P
ost_Jv_Type = 5 Then
'Select Rt_Bill_Ser J_Ser,
Rt_Bill_Doc_Type Jv_type,
Rt_Bill_No J_doc_no,
Rt_Bill_Date J_date,
Cc_code Cs,
From Ias_Rt_Bill_Mst
Where Rt_Bill_Date = Nvl(:P_Date , Rt_Bill_Date)
And Nvl(Rt_Bill_Doc_Type,0) = Decode(:P_Jv_Type,0,Nvl(Rt_Bill_Doc_Type,0),:P_Jv_Type)
And Nvl(Cash_No,0) =Decode(Nvl(:P_Cash_Bank,0) ,0,Nvl(
Cash_No,0),Nvl(:P_Cash_Bank,0) )
And Nvl(Cc_code,''0'') =Decode(:P_Cs,''0'',Nvl(Cc_code,''0''),:P_Cs)
And Nvl(W_Code,0) =Decode(nvl(:P_W_Code,0),0,Nvl(W_Code,0),nvl(:P_W_Code,0))
And Brn_No = Ias_Rt_Bill_Mst.Brn_No
And nvl(Ias_Rt_Bill_Mst.Rt_Bill_Py,0)=0
And Nvl(Rt_Bill_Post,0)=Decode(:P_POST_UNPOST,1,0,1)
And (Nvl(Rt_Bill_Post,0)=0 OR Exists ( Select Doc_Ser From Ias_Post_Mst
and Doc_type = 5
and Doc_Ser=Ias_Rt_Bill_Mst.Rt_Bill_Ser
'||P_Order_By
-- - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - Purchase - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - -
ElsIf P_Post_Jv_Type = 6 Then
Jv_type,
J_date,
Cheque_No,
Ac_Code,
DOC_SEQUENCE ,
gr_no,
G_Ser
From
(Select Ias_pi_bill_Mst.Bill_Ser

From IAS_GRNT_INCR_MST Mj
and Mj.doc_srl = nvl(P_doc_ser, Mj.doc_srl)
Update IAS_GRNT_INCR_MST Set DOC_POST = 1 ,
End Post_Incr_Grnt;
--mmmmmmmmmmmmmmmmmmmmmmmmmmmmmmmmmmmmmmmmmmmmmmmmmmmmmmmmmmmmmmmmmmmmmmmmmmmmmmmmmmmmm
Procedure Post_GRNT_ClS( P_f_doc_no In NUMBER Default Null
-----------------------------------------------------------------------------;
( Select 29 doc_type
,Mj.doc_no_ref
,Mj.Gr_close
,Mj.GR_CLS_DATE
,Mj.doc_srl_ref
Mj.ad_u_id
,Ad_Date_ref Ad_Date
,Up_U_Id_ref Up_U_Id
,Up_Date_ref Up_Date
,Unpost_U_Id_ref Unpost_U_Id
,Unpost_Date_ref
Where nvl(Mj.DOC_POST_REF,0)=Decode(Nvl(P_Post_Type,0),0,0,nvl(Mj.DOC_POST_REF,0))
and Mj.GR_CLOSE = Decode(nvl(P_jv_type,0),0,Mj.GR_CLOSE,P_jv_type)
and Mj.doc_no_ref between nvl(P_f_doc_no,Mj.doc_no_ref) and nvl(P_t_doc_no,Mj.doc_no_ref)
and Mj.GR_CLS_DATE between nvl(P_f_doc_date,Mj.GR_CLS_DATE) and nvl(P_t_doc_date,Mj.GR_CLS_DATE)
and Mj.doc_srl_ref = nvl(P_doc_ser, Mj.doc_srl_ref)
For D_cv in ( Select
Where Dj.doc_srl=M_cv.doc_ser
) --And dj.doc_typ=29
Update IAS_GRNT_MST Set doc_post_ref = 1 ,
Post_u_id_ref = V_User_no,
Post_Date_ref = SysDate
Where DOC_SRL_REF =M_cv.doc_ser;
P_local_
End Post_Grnt_ClS;

,Mv.v_ser doc_ser
,Mv.ad_u_id ad_u_id
,Mv.Brn_no Brn_no
,Mv.Brn_Year Brn_Year
,Mv.CMP_NO
,Mv.BRN_USR
,Audit_Ref_Desc

--and nvl(Mv.Move_Cheq_Py,0) = 0

and Mv.v_ser = nvl(P_doc_ser, Mv.v_ser)
and Mv.Brn_No = nvl(P_Brn_No, Mv.Brn_No)

P_Brn_No => Get_PST_BRN_NO(M_Cv.Brn_No,M_Cv.Doc_Brn_No),--Nvl(M_Cv.Doc_Brn_No,M_Cv.Brn_No) ,
ElsIf M_cv.jv_type = 2 and M_cv.cheq_type = 2 Then --(3) -- Cheque Direct Interface
v_PayRec_ac:=Ias_Posting_Pkg.Get_BnkRecPay_Ac (M_cv.Cash_no,1);
Raise_application_error(-20618,Ias_Gen
_Pkg.Get_Msg(V_Lang_No,799)||SqlErrm) ;
--## v_rec_ltr_ac (DR)
oc_type => M_cv.doc_type,
p_a_code => v_RecLtr_ac,
=> D_Cv.Wo_No,
=> D_Cv.Wo_Ser,
--## v_rec_ltr_ac (CR)
=> Ys_Ac_Dtl_Pkg.GET_AC_DTL_TYP(v_RecLtr_ac) ,
p_amt => -(v_amt),
p_amt_f => -(v_amtf),
p_j_date => D_cv.value_date,
=> D_Cv.Wo_Ser
P_Doc_Brn_No => M_Cv.Doc_Brn_No
--## Get Account From bank MMM

--## bank_ac (Cash_no )(DR)
v_curr_bal:=IAS_Posting_Pkg.Check_Acode (v_bank_ac,M_cv.a_cy);
p_a_code => v_bank_ac,
=> Ys_Ac_Dtl_Pkg.GET_AC_DTL_TYP(v_bank_ac) ,
ElsIf M_cv.jv_type = 2 and M_cv.cheq_type = 3 Then -- Manual cheq
If v_Post_Cheq_type = 0 Then -- Not Effect
=> Ys_Ac_Dtl_Pkg.GET_AC_DTL_TYP(v_cb_ac) ,
--P_Rep_Code => D_cv.Rep_Code,
Else -- Effect
Raise_application_error(-20619,Ias_Gen_Pkg.Get_Msg(V_Lang_No,799)||SqlErrm) ;
p_cheque_no => D_cv.cheque_no,
End If; --(3)
End loop; --(2)
End If; --(End Check Exist)
Update Vouchers Set voucher_post = 1 ,
Where voucher_type=1
and v_ser=M_cv.doc_ser;
Raise_application_error(-20621,'Vouchers, '||Ias_Gen_Pkg.Get_Msg(V_Lang_No,1123)||SqlErrm) ;
If Sql%No
tFound Then
Ias_Posting_Pkg.Update_IntrM_Tab ( P_doc_type => M_cv.doc_type,
P_PJ_NO => M_cv.PJ_NO,
P_ref_no => M_cv.ref_no,
p_ad_u_id => M_cv.ad_u_id,
=> V_User_no,
P_Brn_Year => M_cv.Brn_Year
M_cv.CMP_NO ,
M_cv.BRN_USR);
End Post_Rec;

p_amt => -(D_cv_mst.amt),

If (M_cv.jv_type = 1 ) or (M_cv.jv_type = 2 and M_cv.cheq_type = 0 ) Then --(3)

--## Cach/Bank No (CR)

"PKG INIT"IAS_GL_CSHFLW_PKG""
CREATE OR REPLACE PACKAGE Ias_Gl_CshFlw_Pkg AS
FUNCTION Chk_CshFlw_Fld ( P_Doc_Ser IN NUMBER ,
P_Rcrd_No_Dtl IN NUMBER ,
P_Rcrd_No_Ac IN NUMBER ,
P_Doc_Type IN NUMBER ,
P_Cc_Code IN VARCHAR2 ,
P_Pj_No IN VARCHAR2 ,
P_Actv_No IN VARCHAR2 ,
P_A_Cy IN VARCHAR2 ,
P_Ref_No IN VARCHAR2 ,
P_C_Code IN VARCHAR2 ,
P_V_Code IN VARCHAR2 ,
P_Col_No IN NUMBER ,
P_Rep_Code IN VARCHAR2 ,
P_Brn_No IN NUMBER ) RETURN NUMBER;
PROCEDURE Update_Rcrd_Formula (P_Doc_Ser IN NUMBER ) ;
FUNCTION Get_Rcrd_Dtl_Val ( P_Doc_Ser IN NUMBER ,
P_Grp_Type IN NUMBER ,
P_Prd_Val IN VARCHAR2 ,
P_Fld_Grp IN VARCHAR2 ,
P_Fld_Grp_val IN VARCHAR2 ,
P_Fdate IN DATE ,
P_Tdate IN DATE ,
P_Rcrd_Calc IN VARCHAR2 ,
P_Slct_Doc_Typ IN VARCHAR2,
P_WHR IN VARCHAR2 DEFAULT ' AND 1=1 ' ) RETURN NUMBER;
FUNCTION Get_Rcrd_Dtl_Val_Formula ( P_Doc_Ser IN NUMBER ,
P_Rcrd_Formula IN VARCHAR2 ) RETURN NUMBER;
END Ias_Gl_CshFlw_Pkg;
CREATE OR REPLACE PACKAGE BODY Ias_Gl_CshFlw_Pkg AS
P_Brn_No IN NUMBER ) RETURN NUMBER IS
---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
-------------------------------
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
V_Cnt Number:=1;
For I In (Select Rcrd_No,Fld_Nm From Ias_Gl_Cshflw_fld Where Doc_Ser=P_Doc_Ser And Rcrd_No_Dtl=P_Rcrd_No_Dtl And Rcrd_No_Ac=P_Rcrd_No_Ac) Loop
V_Cnt := Ias_Gen_Pkg.Get_Cnt('Select 1 From Ias_Gl_Cshflw_fld_Dtl
Where Doc_Ser='||P_Doc_Ser||'
And Rcrd_No_Dtl='||P_Rcrd_No_Dtl||'
And Rcrd_No_Ac='||P_Rcrd_No_Ac||'
And Rcrd_No_Fld='||I.Rcrd_No||'
And Fld_val=Decode('''||I.Fld_Nm||''',''DOC_TYPE'','''||P_Doc_Type||''',
''CC_CODE'','''||NVL(P_Cc_Code,'0')||''',
''PJ_NO'','''||NVL(P_Pj_No,'0')||''',
''ACTV_NO'','''||NVL(P_Actv_No,'0')||''',
''A_CY'','''||P_A_Cy||''',
''REF_NO'','''||NVL(P_Ref_No,'0')||''',
''C_CODE'','''||NVL(P_C_Code,'0')||''',
''V_CODE'','''||NVL(P_V_Code,'0')||''',
''COL_NO'','''||NVL(P_Col_No,0)||''',
''REP_CODE'','''||NVL(P_Rep_Code,'0')||''',
''BRN_NO'','''||P_Brn_No||''')
And RowNum<=1');
If Nvl(V_Cnt,0)=0 Then
V_Cnt :=0;
Exit;
RETURN V_Cnt;
END Chk_CshFlw_Fld;
--##############################################################################################################################################
PROCEDURE Update_Rcrd_Formula (P_Doc_Ser IN NUMBER ) IS
Cursor C_CshFlw Is Select Rcrd_No,Rcrd_Formula_Desc,Rcrd_Formula From Ias_Gl_CshFlw_Dtl Where Doc_Ser=P_Doc_Ser And Rcrd_Conn_Type=2 Order By Rcrd_No;
V_Max_Rcrd_No Number;
V_Rcrd_No Number;
V_Rcrd_Formula Varchar2(2000);
Update Ias_Gl_CshFlw_Dtl Set Rcrd_Formula=Null Where Doc_Ser=P_Doc_Ser;
V_Max_Rcrd_No := Ias_Gen_Pkg.Get_Cnt('Select Max(Rcrd_No) From Ias_Gl_CshFlw_Dtl Where Doc_Ser='||P_Doc_Ser);
If Nvl(V_Max_Rcrd_No,0)>0 Then
For I In C_CshFlw Loop
V_Rcrd_No := V_Max_Rcrd_No;
V_Rcrd_Formula := I.Rcrd_Formula_Desc;
Loop
V_Rcrd_Formula := Replace(V_Rcrd_Formula,'R'||V_Rcrd_No,'Get_Rcrd_Val('||V_Rcrd_No||')');
V_Rcrd_No := V_Rcrd_No-1;
Exit When V_Rcrd_No=0;
Update Ias_Gl_CshFlw_Dtl Set Rcrd_Formula=V_Rcrd_Formula Where Doc_Ser=P_Doc_Ser And Rcrd_No=I.Rcrd_No;
For I In (Select Rcrd_No,Rcrd_Formula From Ias_Gl_CshFlw_Dtl Where Doc_Ser=P_Doc_Ser And Rcrd_Conn_Type=2) Loop
For J In ( Select Rcrd_No,Rcrd_Formula From Ias_Gl_CshFlw_Dtl Where Doc_Ser=P_Doc_Ser And Rcrd_Conn_Type=2 And Rcrd_No<>I.Rcrd_No) Loop
If I.Rcrd_Formula Like '%Get_Rcrd_Val('||J.Rcrd_No||')%' Then
Update Ias_Gl_CshFlw_Dtl Set Rcrd_Formula=Replace(Rcrd_Formula,'Get_Rcrd_Val('||J.Rcrd_No||')','('||J.Rcrd_Formula||')') Where Doc_Ser=P_Doc_Ser And Rcrd_No=I.Rcrd_No;
Exception When Others Then Null;
P_Fld_Grp_Val IN VARCHAR2 ,
P_Slct_Doc_Typ IN VARCHAR2 ,
P_WHR IN VARCHAR2 DEFAULT ' AND 1=1 ' ) RETURN NUMBER IS
V_Amt Number:=0;
V_Whr Varchar2(4000);
V_Whr:=P_WHR;
If P_Fld_Grp<>'Null' Then
V_Whr := V_Whr||' And Ias_V_Post_Dtl_OPN_YR.'||P_Fld_Grp||'='''||P_Fld_Grp_Val||'''';
If P_Slct_Doc_Typ Is Not Null Then
V_Whr := V_Whr||' And Ias_V_Post_Dtl_OPN_YR.Doc_Type In '||P_Slct_Doc_Typ;
V_Amt := Ias_Gen_Pkg.Get_Cnt('Select Sum( Case When Ias_Gl_CshFlw_Pkg.Chk_CshFlw_Fld ( Ias_Gl_Cshflw_Ac.Doc_Ser ,
Ias_Gl_Cshflw_Ac.Rcrd_No_Dtl,
Ias_Gl_Cshflw_Ac.Rcrd_No,
Ias_V_Post_Dtl_OPN_YR.Doc_Type,
Ias_V_Post_Dtl_OPN_YR.Cc_Code,
Ias_V_Post_Dtl_OPN_YR.Pj_No,
Ias_V_Post_Dtl_OPN_YR.Actv_No,
Ias_V_Post_Dtl_OPN_YR.A_Cy,
Ias_V_Post_Dtl_OPN_YR.Ref_No,
Ias_V_Post_Dtl_OPN_YR.C_Code,
Ias_V_Post_Dtl_OPN_YR.V_Code,
Ias_V_Post_Dtl_OPN_YR.Col_No,
Ias_V_Post_Dtl_OPN_YR.Rep_Code,
Ias_V_Post_Dtl_OPN_YR.Brn_No)=1 Then '||Nvl(P_Rcrd_Calc,'Amt')||' else 0 End)
From Ias_V_Post_Dtl_OPN_YR,Ias_Acc_Tree,Ias_Gl_Cshflw_Ac
Where Ias_V_Post_Dtl_OPN_YR.A_Code = Ias_Acc_Tree.A_Code
And Ias_Acc_Tree.Ac_Tree = Ias_Gl_Cshflw_Ac.A_Code
And Ias_Gl_Cshflw_Ac.Doc_Ser = '||P_Doc_Ser||'
And Ias_Gl_Cshflw_Ac.Rcrd_No_Dtl = '||P_Rcrd_No_Dtl||'
And Ias_V_Post_Dtl_OPN_YR.Doc_Date Between '''||P_Fdate||''' And '''||P_Tdate||''' '||V_Whr||'
And Decode('||P_Grp_Type||', 1 , TO_CHAR(Ias_V_Post_Dtl_OPN_YR.Doc_Date,''DD/MM/YYYY''),
2 , TO_CHAR(Ias_V_Post_Dtl_OPN_YR.Doc_Da
te,''WW (MM/YYYY)''),
3 , TO_CHAR(Ias_V_Post_Dtl_OPN_YR.Doc_Date,''MM/YYYY''),
4 , TO_CHAR(Ias_V_Post_Dtl_OPN_YR.Doc_Date,''Q/YYYY''),
5 , (CASE WHEN TO_CHAR(Ias_V_Post_Dtl_OPN_YR.Doc_Date,''MM'') IN (''01'',''02'',''03'',''04'') THEN ''01''
WHEN TO_CHAR(Ias_V_Post_Dtl_OPN_YR.Doc_Date,''MM'') IN (''05'',''06'',''07'',''08'') THEN ''02''
WHEN TO_CHAR(Ias_V_Post_Dtl_OPN_YR.Doc_Date,''MM'') IN (''09'',''10'',''11'',''12'') THEN ''03'' END)||''/''||TO_CHAR(Ias_V_Post_Dtl_OPN_YR.Doc_Date,''YYYY''),
6 , (CASE WHEN TO_CHAR(Ias_V_Post_Dtl_OPN_YR.Doc_Date,''Q'') IN (1,2) THEN ''01''
WHEN TO_CHAR(Ias_V_Post_Dtl_OPN_YR.Doc_Date,''Q'') IN (3,4) THEN ''02'' END)||''/''||TO_CHAR(Ias_V_Post_Dtl_OPN_YR.Doc_Date,''YYYY''),
7 , TO_CHAR(Ias_V_Post_Dtl_OPN_YR.Doc_Date,''YYYY''))='''||P_Prd_Val||'''');
RETURN(Nvl(V_Amt,0));
Exception When Others Then RETURN(0);
P_Rcrd_Formula IN VARCHAR2 ) RETURN NUMBER IS
V_Foumula Varchar2(5000);
SELECT Replace(P_Rcrd_Formula,'Get_Rcrd_Val(','(Select Nvl(sum(Blnc_amt),0) From Ias_Gl_CshFlw_Tmp Where Doc_Ser='||P_Doc_Ser||' And Prd='''||P_Prd_Val||''' And Nvl(Fld_Grp,''0'')='''||Nvl(P_Fld_Grp,'0')||''' And Rcrd_No=')
InTo V_Foumula
From Dual;
V_Amt := Ias_Gen_Pkg.Get_Cnt('Select '||V_Foumula||' From Dual');
RETURN(V_Amt);
nvl(P_cash_no, Mv.cash_no)

"PKG INIT"IAS_GET_ALL_FLD_TBL_FNC"P_TBL_NM"C_FLD"<cursor ptr>"COLUMN_NAME"V_FLD"I""

eq_type = 0 Then --(2) Not Effect on account code

PROCEDURE Aud_List_View (
List_Qry IN OUT Aud_List_Rec ,
P_Aud_Jv_Type IN Number,
P_DOC_SEQUENCE IN Number DEFAULT 0) IS
If P_Aud_Jv_Type = 1 Then
Ias_Gen_Pkg.Get_Local_Cur A_CY ,
Nvl(Batch_val,0) amt ,
Nvl(Audit_Ref,0) Audit_Ref,
AUDIT_REF_DESC
Where Nvl(Jv_Type,0) = Decode(:P_Jv_Type,0,Nvl(Jv_Type,0),:P_Jv_Type)
And j_date between NVL(:P_F_date,j_date) And NVL(:P_T_date,j_date)
and j_doc_no between nvl(:P_F_Doc_No,J_DOC_NO) AND nvl(:P_T_Doc_No,J_DOC_NO)
And Nvl(Audit_Ref,0)<>1
P_F_date,
P_T_date,
P_F_Doc_No,
P_T_Doc_No,
P_User_No;
ElsIf P_Aud_Jv_Type = 2 Then
If nvl(P_CHQ_DUE,0) = 0 Then
Null W_Code,
A_Cy,
Decode(A_Cy,Ias_Gen_Pkg.Get_Local_Cur,CASH_AMT,CASH_AMTf) amt,
And voucher_date between NVL(:P_F_date,Voucher_Date) And NVL(:P_T_date,Voucher_Date)
And voucher_no between nvl(:P_F_Doc_No,Voucher_No) AND nvl(:P_T_Doc_No,Voucher_No)
P_Cs ,
ELSIF P_CHQ_DUE = 1 Then
VALUE_DATE J_date,
Decode(A_Cy,Ias_Gen_Pkg.Get_Local_Cur,AC_AMT,AC_AMTf) amt,
DOC_SEQUENCE DOC_SEQUENCE,
And CHEQUE_VALUED in(1,2)
And VALUE_DATE between NVL(:P_F_date,VALUE_DATE) And NVL(:P_T_date,VALUE_DATE)
And voucher_no betwe
en nvl(:P_F_Doc_No,Voucher_No) AND nvl(:P_T_Doc_No,Voucher_No)
And Brn_No = Voucher_Detail.Brn_No
And Exists (Select 1 From Vouchers
Where V_ser = Voucher_Detail.V_ser
And Nvl(CHEQ_TYPE,0) = 3 And RowNum <= 1 )
ElsIf P_Aud_Jv_Type = 3 Then
here U_Id = :P_User_No
ElsIf P_Aud_Jv_Type = 4 Then
BILL_CURRENCY A_CY,
BILL_AMT Amt,
Nvl(Audit_Ref,0) Audit_Ref ,
Where Nvl(Bill_Doc_Type,0) =Decode(:P_Jv_Type,0,Nvl(Bill_Doc_Type,0),:P_Jv_Type)
And Bill_date between NVL(:P_F_date,Bill_Date) And NVL(:P_T_date,Bill_Date)
And Bill_No between nvl(:P_F_Doc_No,Bill_No) AND nvl(:P_T_Doc_No,Bill_No)
Using P_Jv_Type,
ElsIf P_Aud_Jv_Type = 5 Then
RT_BILL_CURRENCY A_CY,
Where Nvl(Rt_Bill_Doc_Type,0) = Decode(:P_Jv_Type,0,Nvl(Rt_Bill_Doc_Type,0),:P_Jv_Type)
And Nvl(STAND_BY,0)=0
And Rt_Bill_date between NVL(:P_F_date,Rt_Bill_Date) And NVL(:P_T_date,Rt_Bill_Date)
And Rt_Bill_No b
etween nvl(:P_F_Doc_No,Rt_Bill_No) AND nvl(:P_T_Doc_No,Rt_Bill_No)
And Nvl(Add_Flag,1) = 1 And RowNum <= 1 ) '||P_Order_By
---------------------------------- Purchase -------- - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - -
ElsIf P_Aud_Jv_Type = 6 Then
Gr_Note.w_code W_Code,
Ias_pi_bill_Mst.Cc_code Cs,
Nvl(Ias_pi_bill_Mst.Audit_Ref,0) Audit_Ref,
Ias_pi_bill_Mst.AUDIT_REF_DESC
And Nvl(Gr_Note.Pending,0) = 0
And Nvl(Ias_pi_bill_Mst.HUNG,0)=0
And Nvl(Bill_Doc_Type,0) = Decode(:P_Jv_Type,0,Nvl(Bill_Doc_Type,0),:P_Jv_Type)
And Nvl(Ias_pi_bill_Mst.Audit_Ref,0)<>1
And Ias_pi_bill_Mst.Bill_date between NVL(:P_F_date,Bill_Date) And NVL(:P_T_date,Bill_Date)

CREATE OR REPLACE PROCEDURE Chk_Bdgt_Blnc_Prc(P_Chk_Amt In number,
P_A_CODE Account.a_code%type,
P_A_Cy Ex_rate.CUR_CODE%type,
P_Cc_Code Cost_centers.cc_CODE%type,
P_PJ_NO Ias_projects.pj_no%type,
P_Actv_NO Ias_Actvty.actv_no%type,
P_DATE IN DATE DEFAULT IAS_GEN_PKG.Get_Sysdate,
P_User_No In Number,
P_BGT_Amt_L out Number,
P_BGT_Amt_F
out Number,
P_BAL out Number,
P_Pass_Prv out Number,
P_Msg out Varchar2 ,
P_Lng In Number,
P_Bgt_Srl In Number Default 0) IS
V_Bal Number:=0;
V_USE_MULTI_CUR_BDGT Number:=0;
V_Frc_No Number:=0;
V_Chk_Bgt_Amt Number:=0;
V_BGT_CUR Number:=0;
V_local_Cur Varchar2(10);
V_F_Date Date;
V_T_Date Date;
---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- PURPOSE: to Check the Budget Balance
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
--##----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------##--
V_local_Cur:=Ys_Gen_Pkg.Get_local_Cur;
Select Nvl(USE_MULTI_CUR_BDGT,0),Nvl(Chk_Bgt_Amt,0),Nvl(NO_OF_DECIMAL,2)
Into V_USE_MULTI_CUR_BDGT,V_Chk_Bgt_Amt, V_Frc_No
From ias_para_gl ;
P_Pass_Prv:=1;
-----hare
--## P_Pass_Prv 1= not allow ,2=allow ,3=allow with worning
Select Nvl(BGT_LMT_PRV,1)
Into P_Pass_Prv
From Privilege_Fixed
Where u_id=P_User_No;
Select Round((Nvl(Amt_L,0)+Nvl(Amt_L_Add,0)+Nvl(Amt_L_Add_Mov,0)-Nvl(Amt_L_Disc,0)-Nvl(Amt_L_Disc_Mov,0)),V_Frc_no),
Round((Nvl(Amt_F,0)+Nvl(Amt_F_Add,0)+Nvl(Amt_F_Add_Mov,0)-Nvl(Amt_F_Disc,0)-Nvl(Amt_F_Disc_Mov,0)),V_Frc_no),
F_Date,
T_Date
Into P_BGT_Amt_L,
P_BGT_Amt_F,
V_F_Date,
V_T_Date
From IAS_BDGT_BLNC_MST M,IAS_BDGT_BLNC_DTL D
where M.DOC_SER=D.doc_ser
AND M.DFLT=Decode(P_Bgt_Srl,0,1,M.DFLT)
and M.DOC_SER=Decode(P_Bgt_Srl,0,M.DOC_SER,P_Bgt_Srl)
AND A_CODE= P_A_CODE
And Nvl(d.a_cy,'0')= Decode(V_USE_MULTI_CUR_BDGT,0,Nvl(d.a_cy,'0'),P_A_Cy)
And P_Date betwe
en F_Date and T_Date
And nvl(cc_code,'0') = Decode(nvl(cc_code,'0'),'0',nvl(cc_code,'0'),P_cc_code)
And nvl(pj_no,'0') = Decode(nvl(pj_no,'0'),'0',nvl(pj_no,'0'),P_PJ_NO)
And nvl(actv_no,'0') = Decode(nvl(actv_no,'0'),'0',nvl(actv_no,'0'),P_ACTV_NO)
Exception when Others Then
P_BGT_Amt_L :=0;
P_BGT_Amt_F:=0;
--## Balance
If V_USE_MULTI_CUR_BDGT=0 THEN
V_BGT_CUR:=1;
V_BGT_CUR:=0;
V_Bal:=Round(Ys_Get_Blnc_Ac_Dtl_Fnc(P_A_CODE =>P_A_CODE,
P_AC_DTL_TYP => 0,
P_AC_CODE_DTL => NULL,
P_LOC_CUR =>V_local_Cur,
P_CUR_CODE =>P_A_CY,
P_CC_CODE =>P_CC_CODE,
P_PJ_CODE =>P_PJ_NO,
P_ACTV_CODE =>P_ACTV_NO,
P_F_DATE =>V_F_DATE,
P_T_DATE =>V_T_DATE,
P_BLNC_TYP => 2,
P_LOC_AMT => V_BGT_CUR ,
P_DOC_POST => 2 ),V_frc_no);
P_Bal:=Nvl(V_Bal,0);
--## BGT_AMT_L
IF V_Chk_Bgt_Amt=1 THEN ---(1)
If V_USE_MULTI_CUR_BDGT=0 Or (V_USE_MULTI_CUR_BDGT=1 and P_A_Cy=V_local_Cur ) Then
IF P_BGT_AMT_L Is Not Null And nvl(V_Bal,0)+Nvl(P_Chk_Amt,0)>P_BGT_AMT_L Then
P_Msg:=Ias_Gen_Pkg.Get_Prompt(P_Lng,6074)||' = '||P_BGT_AMT_L||' '||
Ias_Gen_Pkg.Get_Prompt(P_Lng,139)||' = '||Round((nvl(V_Bal,0)+NVL(P_Chk_Amt,0)),V_Frc_No)||
Ias_Gen_Pkg.Get_Prompt(P_Lng,999)||' = '||Round(abs(P_BGT_AMT_L-(nvl(V_Bal,0)+NVL(P_Chk_Amt,0))),V_Frc_No)||' , '||
Ias_Gen_Pkg.Get_Prompt(P_Lng,3604)||' = '||Abs(Round(((nvl(P_BGT_AMT_L,0)-Nvl(NVL(P_Chk_Amt,0),0)-(nvl(nvl(V_Bal,0),0))) /nvl(P_BGT_AMT_L,0))*100,2));
ElsIf V_USE_MULTI_CUR_BDGT=1 And P_A_Cy<>V_local_Cur Then
IF P_BGT_AMT_F Is Not Null And nvl(V_Bal,0)+Nvl(P_Chk_Amt,0)>P_BGT_AMT_F Then
P_Msg:=Ias_Gen_Pkg.Get_Prompt(P_Lng,6074)||' = '||P_BGT_AMT_F||' '||
Ias_Gen_Pkg.Get_Prompt(P_Lng,999)||' = '||Round(abs(P_BGT_AMT_F-(nvl(V_Bal,0)+NVL(P_Chk_Amt,0))),V_Frc_No)||' , '||
Ias_Gen_Pkg.Get_Prompt(P_Lng,3604)||' = '||Abs(Round(((nvl(P_BGT_AMT_F,0)-Nvl(NVL(P_Chk_Amt,0),0)-(nvl(nvl(V_Bal,0),0))) /nvl(P_BGT_AMT_F,0))*100,2));
End If;---(1)
End Chk_Bdgt_Blnc_Prc;
"PKG INIT"CHK_UPD_CHQNO_PRC""
CREATE OR REPLACE Procedure Chk_Upd_ChqNo_Prc (P_TRS_TYP IN Varchar2,
P_bank_no IN Number,
P_Cur_Code IN Varchar2,
P_Cheque_No IN Varchar2,
P_DOC_NO IN Number,
P_DOC_DATE IN DATE ,
P_DOC_SRL IN Number,
P_DOC_TYP IN Number,
P_DOC_JV_TYP IN Number,
P_CHQ_ST out Number,
P_Msg out varchar2 ,
P_lng_no IN Number default 1) IS
V_Cnt Number;
V_CHQ_ST Number;
V_DOC_NO_REF Number;
V_DOC_SRL_REF Number;
V_DOC_TYP_REF Number;
V_DOC_JV_TYP_REF Number;
V_TBL_NM Varchar2(60);
V_DOC_SRL Varchar2(60);
V_CHQ_NO Number;
V_DUP_CHQ_PRMTR Number;
V_DUP_CHQ_PRMTR:=Nvl(IAS_GEN_PKG.GET_FLD_VALUE('IAS_PARA_GL','NVL(DUP_CHQ_NO_PAYMNT,0)'),0);
IF P_DOC_TYP=3 THEN
V_TBL_NM:='VOUCHER_DETAIL';
V_DOC_SRL:='V_SER';
ELSIF P_DOC_TYP=6 THEN
V_TBL_NM:='IAS_PI_BILL_MST';
V_DOC_SRL:='BILL_SER';
Select 1
Into V_Cnt
from GLS_BNK_CHQ_MST
where bank_no = p_bank_no
And CUR_CODE = P_Cur_Code
V_Cnt:=0;
If NVL(V_Cnt,0) = 1 THEN
from GLS_BNK_CHQ_DTL
where bank_no = P_bank_no
And CHQ_NO = P_CHEQUE_NO
P_Msg:= YS_GEN_PKG.Get_Msg(P_Lng_no ,1935)||' '||IAS_CSHBNK_PKG.GET_CB_NAME(2,P_bank_no,P_Lng_no);
Return;
------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
If NVL(V_Cnt,0) = 1 Then
Select CHQ_ST ,CHQ_NO,DOC_NO_REF,DOC_TYP_REF,DOC_JV_TYP_REF,DOC_SRL_REF
Into V_CHQ_ST,V_CHQ_NO,V_DOC_NO_REF,V_DOC_TYP_REF,V_DOC_JV_TYP_REF,V_DOC_SRL_REF
And CHQ_NO =P_CHEQUE_NO
V_CHQ_ST:=1;
P_CHQ_ST:=V_CHQ_ST;
IF NVL(V_CHQ_ST,1)=2 AND P_TRS_TYP IN ('I','U') then --AND P_DUP_CHQ=0 THEN --USED
IF V_DUP_CHQ_PRMTR=1 AND P_DOC_TYP=3 AND NVL(V_DOC_SRL_REF,0)<>NVL(P_DOC_SRL,0) THEN
P_MSG:= YS_GEN_PKG.Get_Msg(P_Lng_no ,763); --763
ELSIF V_DUP_CHQ_PRMTR=1 AND P_DOC_TYP=3 AND NVL(V_DOC_SRL_REF,0)=NVL(P_DOC_SRL,0) THEN
NULL;
P_MSG:= YS_GEN_PKG.Get_Msg(P_Lng_no ,763);
ELSIF NVL(V_CHQ_ST,1)=3 THEN -- CANCELED
P_Msg:= YS_GEN_PKG.Get_Msg(P_Lng_no ,1936);
ELSIF NVL(V_CHQ_ST,1)=4 THEN -- STOPPED
P_Msg:= YS_GEN_PKG.Get_Msg(P_Lng_no ,1937);
IF NVL(V_CHQ_ST,1)=1 AND P_TRS_TYP IN ('I','U') THEN
UPDATE GLS_BNK_CHQ_DTL SET CHQ_ST = 2,
DOC_NO_REF = P_DOC_NO,
DOC_DATE_REF = P_DOC_DATE,
DOC_SRL_REF = P_DOC_SRL,
DOC_TYP_REF = P_DOC_TYP,
DOC_JV_TYP_REF = P_DOC_JV_TYP
WHERE Bank_No = P_bank_no
AND CHQ_NO = P_CHEQUE_NO;
IF NVL(V_CHQ_ST,1) =2 AND P_TRS_TYP ='U' THEN
UPDATE GLS_BNK_CHQ_DTL SET DOC_DATE_REF = P_DOC_DATE,
AND DOC_SRL_REF = P_DOC_SRL
If V_CHQ_ST =2 AND P_TRS_TYP ='D' THEN
UPDATE GLS_BNK_CHQ_DTL SET CHQ_ST = 1,
DOC_NO_REF = Null,
DOC_DATE_REF = Null,
DOC_SRL_REF = Null,
DOC_TYP_REF = Null,
DOC_JV_TYP_REF = Null
WHERE bank_no = p_bank_no
And DOC_SRL_REF = V_DOC_SRL_REF
End Chk_Upd_ChqNo_Prc;
j_date => M_cv.doc_date,
$p%@.
GLS_INSERT_FLG_PRC
GLS_INSERT_LOV_PRC
GLS_INSERT_LST_PRC
-p.@:

Return(V_Doc_No);

END Get_Doc_No;
